好，给你“能落地、能自测、能扩展”的一套最小闭环。直接照做就能跑通 OKX 本地验证 + 证据链返回；合约上链留好接口。

# 明日可落地清单（90–180 分钟）
1) 起本地校验服务（FastAPI，127.0.0.1:8082）  
2) 接 OKX 私有 REST（签名/HMAC），只做**订单校验**基础闭环  
3) 产出**统一证据 JSON**（含 raw、normalized、merkle、perf）并落盘  
4) SQLite 建表 & 存储 verify 结果（不落明文密钥）  
5) 前端按钮调用 `/api/verify`，页面展示 JSON，另给“复制 Merkle Root”按钮  
6) 预留 `/api/attest/preview` 输出上链负载（不真正上链）

---

# 统一接口与字段规范

## 请求：`POST /api/verify`
用于本地验证 OKX 订单 + 返回证据链

```json
{
  "exchange": "okx",
  "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
  "live": true,
  "fresh": true,
  "noCache": true,

  "auth": {
    "mode": "inline",               // inline | ref
    "apiKey": "xxxx",               // mode=inline 时必填
    "secretKey": "xxxx",
    "passphrase": "xxxx",
    "uid": "optional"               // 有则传
    /* 或者:
    "mode": "ref",
    "keysId": "k_01J...ABC"         // 数据库里已保存的密钥引用
    */
  },

  "options": {
    "includeRaw": true,             // 是否回显原始 OKX 响应体（会敏感脱敏）
    "returnFields": ["order","fills","liquidation"],
    "timeoutMs": 12000
  }
}
```

> 只做 **订单校验闭环**：最核心是能打通签名请求、拿到订单详情 + 成交明细，再做规范化和证据链生成。  
> “爆仓/不爆仓”先作为 **信号集** 输出（见下文），等你提供更精确字段再细化。

## 响应（证据链）：`200 OK`
下面是**字段清单**（严格固定，前端/合约统一依赖），示例为精简版，真实会更长：

```json
{
  "verifyId": "v7_2025-11-04T01:23:45.678Z_5f2c...",   // UUIDv7 或等价可排序ID
  "verifier": {
    "version": "0.1.0",
    "host": "localhost",
    "startedAt": "2025-11-04T01:23:45.120Z",
    "finishedAt": "2025-11-04T01:23:45.740Z",
    "durationMs": 620
  },
  "query": {
    "exchange": "okx",
    "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
    "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
    "live": true,
    "fresh": true,
    "noCache": true
  },
  "normalized": {
    "order": {
      "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
      "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
      "state": "filled",           // filled | live | canceled | rejected | ...
      "side": "sell",              // buy | sell
      "ordType": "market",         // market | limit | post_only | fok | ioc ...
      "px": null,                  // 限价单有值
      "sz": "1.00",
      "accFillSz": "1.00",         // 累计成交量
      "avgPx": "65000.5",
      "cTime":  "1727777777000",   // 创建毫秒时间戳（字符串原样）
      "uTime":  "1727777790000",
      "tag":    "",                // 用户自定义标签（如有）
      "reduceOnly": true,          // 如有返回则保留；没有则 null
      "posSide": "short",          // long | short | net | null
      "lever": "20",               // 杠杆，如有
      "feeCcy": "USDT",
      "fee": "-1.23"
    },
    "fills": [
      {
        "tradeId": "1234567890",
        "px": "65010.0",
        "sz": "0.64",
        "fee": "-0.45",
        "ts": "1727777788000"
      }
    ],
    "liquidation": {
      "status": "unknown",         // liquidated | not_liquidated | adl | unknown
      "signals": [
        /* 规则命中记录（字符串），例如：
           "FILLS_MARKET_CLOSE",
           "ORDER_REDUCEONLY_TO_ZERO",
           "POSITION_SIZE_DROP_SPIKE",
           "OKX_SPECIFIC_FIELD_XYZ"
        */
      ],
      "reason": null,              // 有明确理由时填，例如 "ADL" / "MARGIN_CALL"
      "window": { "from": "...", "to": "..." }  // 用于日保/时段保
    }
  },
  "decision": {
    "status": "VERIFIED",          // VERIFIED | NOT_FOUND | AUTH_FAIL | NETWORK_ERROR | BAD_REQUEST
    "score": 1.0,                  // 规则分（可选）
    "rationale": [
      "ORDER_FOUND",
      "FILLS_CONSISTENT"
    ],
    "rulesVersion": "r0.1"
  },
  "raw": {
    "okx": {
      "order": { "...": "原始字段（脱敏）" },
      "fills": [{ "...": "原始字段（脱敏）" }]
    },
    "http": {
      "latencyMs": 180,
      "retries": 0
    }
  },
  "evidence": {
    "leaves": [
      { "k": "query", "h": "sha256:..." },
      { "k": "raw.okx.order", "h": "sha256:..." },
      { "k": "normalized.order", "h": "sha256:..." },
      { "k": "normalized.fills", "h": "sha256:..." },
      { "k": "decision", "h": "sha256:..." }
    ],
    "algorithm": "keccak256",
    "leafCount": 5,
    "root": "0xabc...def",
    "parentRoot": null            // 可选：串联祖先根
  },
  "perf": {
    "totalMs": 620,
    "okxMs": 180,
    "computeMs": 40,
    "cache": false
  },
  "storage": {
    "file": "/var/lib/liqpass/evidence/2025-11-04/okx_BTC-USDT-SWAP_2940071038556348417_v7.json",
    "sha256": "eee...",
    "size": 24567
  }
}
```

> 关键点：**字段名固定**，将 OKX 多而杂的返回统一收敛到 `normalized.*`；原始体放 `raw.okx.*`；上链只用 `evidence.root` + `verifyId` 等少量字段。

## 上链预览：`POST /api/attest/preview`
输入 `verifyId`，回出**合约调用负载**（不签名、不广播）：

```json
{
  "verifyId": "v7_...",
  "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
  "root": "0xabc...def",
  "parentRoot": null,
  "timestamp": 1730654725,
  "commit": "git:5d2c9ab",
  "typedDataV4": {
    "domain": {...},
    "types":  {...},
    "message": {
      "verifyId": "v7_...",
      "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
      "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
      "root": "0xabc...def",
      "parentRoot": "0x000...000",
      "timestamp": 1730654725
    }
  }
}
```

# SQLite 表结构（最小集）

```sql
-- 保存密钥引用（不存明文）
CREATE TABLE api_keys (
  id            TEXT PRIMARY KEY,             -- k_01J...
  owner         TEXT,                         -- 本地用户/机器
  exchange      TEXT CHECK (exchange='okx'),
  key_id_tail   TEXT,                         -- apiKey后4位，便于识别
  enc_secret    BLOB NOT NULL,                -- AES-256-GCM 密文
  enc_pass      BLOB,                         -- 有 passphrase 的情况
  created_at    TEXT,
  last_used_at  TEXT,
  active        INTEGER DEFAULT 1
);

-- 验证主表
CREATE TABLE verifications (
  verify_id     TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  exchange      TEXT,
  instId        TEXT,
  ordId         TEXT,
  status        TEXT,                         -- 同 decision.status
  root          TEXT,                         -- evidence.root
  parent_root   TEXT,
  result_json   TEXT,                         -- 全量证据 JSON（或拆表）
  created_at    TEXT
);

-- 证据文件索引（可选）
CREATE TABLE evidence_files (
  id            INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  verify_id     TEXT,
  path          TEXT,
  sha256        TEXT,
  size          INTEGER,
  created_at    TEXT
);
```

**密钥安全**  
- 生成 `.env` 中的 `LP_MASTER_KEY`（32 字节 base64），用 AES-256-GCM 加密 `secretKey/passphrase`；只保存密文。  
- 日志**不**打印密钥/签名头；`raw.okx.*` 里对敏感字段做脱敏（中间几位替换为 `***`）。

---

# 自测脚本（一次过）

```bash
# 1) 健康
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8082/healthz | jq .

# 2) 直接验证（inline密钥）
curl -sS -m 20 -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{
  "exchange":"okx",
  "instId":"BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "ordId":"2940071038556348417",
  "live":true,"fresh":true,"noCache":true,
  "auth":{"mode":"inline","apiKey":"__KEY__","secretKey":"__SEC__","passphrase":"__PP__"},
  "options":{"includeRaw":true,"timeoutMs":12000}
}' http://127.0.0.1:8082/api/verify | jq . | tee /tmp/evidence.json

# 3) 提取 Merkle Root
jq -r '.evidence.root' /tmp/evidence.json

# 4) 上链预览负载（不签名）
VID=$(jq -r '.verifyId' /tmp/evidence.json)
curl -sS -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d "{\"verifyId\":\"$VID\"}" \
  http://127.0.0.1:8082/api/attest/preview | jq .
```

**预期**  
- `decision.status = "VERIFIED"`  
- 存在 `normalized.order/accFillSz/avgPx/...`  
- 得到 `evidence.root`，且 `/var/lib/liqpass/evidence/…` 有对应文件

---

# 前端集成（按钮点击）
```ts
// 假设本地后端同域代理到 127.0.0.1:8082
import axios from 'axios';

async function verifyOkx(form: {
  instId: string; ordId: string; apiKey: string; secretKey: string; passphrase: string;
}) {
  const { data } = await axios.post('/api/verify', {
    exchange: 'okx',
    instId: form.instId,
    ordId: form.ordId,
    live: true, fresh: true, noCache: true,
    auth: { mode: 'inline', apiKey: form.apiKey, secretKey: form.secretKey, passphrase: form.passphrase },
    options: { includeRaw: true, timeoutMs: 12000 }
  });
  return data; // 展示 data.normalized / data.evidence.root
}
```

---

# “爆仓 vs 不爆仓”现在如何处理？
- 先**不硬编码**某个厂商专有字段，统一输出到 `normalized.liquidation`：  
  - `status`: `liquidated | not_liquidated | adl | unknown`  
  - `signals`: 命中的规则字符串数组（如 `FILLS_MARKET_CLOSE`、`POSITION_ZERO_AFTER_MARGIN_CALL` 等）  
- 你明天把**更具体的 OKX 字段**贴我（比如能明确指示 ADL/强平的字段/接口），我把规则完善成可复现的判定，并且**只改规则表，不改响应结构**。

---

# 为什么这套更踏实？
- **字段固定**：前端、合约、审计三方都对同一个 JSON 契约编程。  
- **可渐进**：先打通“订单可验证 + 证据可落盘 + Merkle 可计算”，再细化爆仓规则。  
- **安全默认值**：不落明文、不打印密钥、默认本地监听。  
- **上链解耦**：`/api/attest/preview` 先稳定负载格式，上链晚点再联调。

如果你愿意，我可以把这套接口契约再配一份**最小 FastAPI 代码骨架**（含 OKX 签名、SQLite、Merkle 计算与落盘），你直接粘贴运行即可。

收到截图了。你的“证据包”目录结构和字段我看明白了：`bills.json / fills.json / index.json / instrument.json / meta.json / order.json / merkle.json / summary.json`。下面给你**一套精确字段对齐表**（OKX 原字段 → 统一 normalized 字段），再给**强平/非强平判定规则（最小可用版）**，直接按这个收口，前端两个按钮就能跑全闭环。

# 1) 字段映射：OKX → normalized

## A. order.json（订单）
常见 OKX 字段（你已有/OKX 会给）：
- `ordId, clOrdId, instId, instType, state, side, ordType, px, sz, accFillSz, avgPx, cTime, uTime, tag, reduceOnly, tdMode, posSide, lever, fee, feeCcy, rebate, rebateCcy`

统一到：
```json
normalized.order = {
  "ordId":        ordId,
  "clOrdId":      clOrdId || null,
  "instId":       instId,
  "instType":     instType,             // SWAP / FUTURES / MARGIN / SPOT
  "state":        state,                // live/filled/canceled/rejected/...
  "side":         side,                 // buy/sell
  "posSide":      posSide || null,      // long/short/net
  "ordType":      ordType,              // market/limit/fok/ioc/post_only/...
  "tdMode":       tdMode || null,       // cross/isolated
  "reduceOnly":   reduceOnly ?? null,
  "px":           px ?? null,
  "sz":           sz,
  "accFillSz":    accFillSz || "0",
  "avgPx":        avgPx || null,
  "lever":        lever || null,
  "fee":          fee || null,
  "feeCcy":       feeCcy || null,
  "rebate":       rebate || null,
  "rebateCcy":    rebateCcy || null,
  "tag":          tag || "",
  "cTime":        cTime,                // 原样毫秒字符串
  "uTime":        uTime
}
```

## B. fills.json（成交明细）
你截图里已有字段（示例）：
`fillSz, fee, ordId, posSide, execType:"M/T", fillIdxPx, side, fillPx, fillPnl, instType:"SWAP", instId, billId, subType:"3", fillTime, tradeId, fillMarkPx, feeCcy:"USDT", ts ...`
再补常见可能出现的：`fillPxUsd, tradeQuoteCcy, cOrdId, tag, fillFwdPx, fillPxVol, fillMarkVol ...`

统一到：
```json
normalized.fills[] = {
  "tradeId":   tradeId,
  "billId":    billId || null,          // 能关联到账变更
  "side":      side,                     // buy/sell
  "posSide":   posSide || null,          // long/short/net
  "execType":  execType,                 // M(做市)/T(吃单)
  "px":        fillPx,                   // 成交价
  "pxIdx":     fillIdxPx || null,        // 指数价
  "pxMark":    fillMarkPx || null,       // 标记价
  "sz":        fillSz,
  "pnl":       fillPnl || null,
  "fee":       fee || null,
  "feeCcy":    feeCcy || null,
  "instId":    instId,
  "instType":  instType,
  "subType":   subType || null,          // OKX 内部分类，保留
  "ts":        (fillTime || ts)          // 统一时间字段
}
```

## C. bills.json（账变/结算）
用于识别**强平/ADL/保险基金**等事件；常见字段（OKX 账单里会见到）：
`billId, ts, type, subType, instId, instType, px, sz, pnl, fee, feeCcy, balChg, posBalChg, ccy ...`

统一到：
```json
normalized.bills[] = {
  "billId":   billId,
  "ts":       ts,
  "type":     type,          // 保留原码
  "subType":  subType || null,
  "instId":   instId || null,
  "instType": instType || null,
  "px":       px || null,
  "sz":       sz || null,
  "pnl":      pnl || null,
  "fee":      fee || null,
  "feeCcy":   feeCcy || null,
  "balChg":   balChg || null,
  "posBalChg":posBalChg || null
}
```

## D. instrument.json / index.json
- instrument：`instId, instType, lotSz, ctVal, ctMult, ctValCcy, tickSz, minSz, state ...`
- index：`ts, idxPx`（如有 K 线或多条就数组）

统一到：
```json
normalized.instrument = { ...保留主要合约元数据... }
normalized.index[]    = [{ "ts": ts, "idxPx": idxPx }]
```

## E. merkle.json / meta.json / summary.json
- `merkle.json`：`algorithm, leaves[{k,h}], root, leafCount, parentRoot?`
- `meta.json`：请求/响应耗时、IP、UA、签名版本、缓存命中等
- `summary.json`：你现在的汇总统计（可继续输出 decision、signals、root 摘要）

---

# 2) “强平 / 不强平”判定（最小可用规则）

> 目标：**先给出可落地且可审计的判定**。等你把 OKX 某些“强平/ADL 专用字段或账单 code”贴出来，再把规则表精化；但**输出结构不变**（前端/合约不需要改）。

在证据里输出：
```json
normalized.liquidation = {
  "status": "liquidated | adl | not_liquidated | unknown",
  "signals": [ "BILLS_TYPE_LIQUIDATION", "ORDER_REDUCEONLY_TO_ZERO", "FILLS_MARKET_CLOSE", ... ],
  "reason": "LIQUIDATION | ADL | USER_CLOSE | null",
  "window": { "from": "...", "to": "..." }
}
```

判定流程（按优先级）：
1) **账单直证据（最高优先级）**  
   - 若 `normalized.bills` 中存在任意记录能明确指示强平/ADL（通常通过 `type/subType` 码值），  
     → `status = liquidated` 或 `adl`，`signals += ["BILLS_TYPE_xxx"]`，`reason = "LIQUIDATION" | "ADL"`。
2) **仓位归零 + 被动平仓迹象（次高）**  
   - 若能拿到“前/后”净仓（可从账变/仓位接口推导 `posBalChg` 或累计 fills），并且在短窗内由非零→零，且**无用户取消/限价未触发痕迹**，  
     → `status = liquidated`，`signals += ["POSITION_ZERO_SPIKE"]`。  
   - 若由用户方向性成交闭合（order.reduceOnly=true 或 全部成交方向与持仓相反且分笔非异常价）  
     → `status = not_liquidated`，`signals += ["USER_REDUCE_CLOSED"]`。
3) **仅有成交但无充分证据** → `status = unknown`，保留所有原始账变/成交叶子，交由人工复核或后续规则更新。

> 说明：OKX 的“强平/ADL”最稳的是**账变类型码**；你已有 `bills.json`，把能代表强平/ADL 的 `type/subType` 直接映射到信号即可。后续你把**具体码表**发我，我把规则表补齐。

---

# 3) 与你现有文件对齐（如何落叶）

把你现在的每个文件进叶：

- `leaves += hash("raw.okx.order", canonical(order.json))`
- `leaves += hash("raw.okx.fills", canonical(fills.json))`
- （若存在）`leaves += hash("raw.okx.bills", canonical(bills.json))`
- `leaves += hash("normalized.order", canonical(normalized.order))`
- `leaves += hash("normalized.fills", canonical(normalized.fills))`
- `leaves += hash("normalized.bills", canonical(normalized.bills))`（有则加）
- `leaves += hash("normalized.liquidation", canonical(normalized.liquidation))`
- `leaves += hash("decision", canonical(decision))`

> canonical：JSON 严格排序、UTF-8、数字保留为**字符串**（避免浮点不同环境差异），hash 用 `keccak256`（或你已用的算法保持一致）。

---

# 4) 前端两个按钮如何打通（与你现在结构一致）

- **按钮A：验证** → `POST /api/verify`（我上一条已给完整请求体）。  
  - 展示：`normalized.order / normalized.fills` 表格 + `normalized.liquidation.status` + `evidence.root` + “复制 root”
- **按钮B：证据上链预览** → `POST /api/attest/preview`  
  - 入参：`{ verifyId }`  
  - 展示：`typedDataV4` + `root` + `ordId/instId/timestamp`（用于钱包签名/合约调试）

---

# 5) 你马上可做的最小改动（10 项）
1. 写一层 **OKX→normalized** 的映射（按上面对齐表）。
2. `normalized.liquidation` 先实现 3 条规则（账变优先、仓位归零、未知）。
3. `evidence.leaves` 固定 key 顺序，生成 `merkle.json` 与 `summary.json`。
4. `/api/verify` 返回统一证据 JSON；把 `raw` 做敏感脱敏（密钥永不回显）。
5. `/api/attest/preview` 产出上链负载（不签名不广播）。
6. SQLite 里 `verifications(root, verify_id, ordId, instId, created_at)` 索引好。
7. 证据文件路径：`/var/lib/liqpass/evidence/YYYY-MM-DD/{instId}_{ordId}_{verifyId}.json`
8. 把你截图里的 `fills.json/bills.json/order.json` 都纳入 `raw.okx.*`。
9. 在 `summary.json` 写入 `decision.status, evidence.root, file.path, sha256`。
10. 前端展示 `normalized.*` + `evidence.root`；按钮B用 `verifyId` 取 `/api/attest/preview`。

---

需要的话我可以把**最小 FastAPI 路由 + 映射骨架**也给你（可直接粘贴运行）。你先按这份对齐表把现有 JSON 收口到 `normalized.*`，`liquidation` 先走账变优先策略，明天把 OKX 的 `type/subType` 码表贴我，我把规则升级到“强平/ADL 准确识别”，但**不改变响应结构**。

最小补齐清单（马上能加）：
1) **instType**（SWAP/FUTURES/SPOT/MARGIN）  
2) **feeCcy**（你现在只有手续费数值，建议补 `USDT`）

5) **fills 补位**：`pxIdx(px 的指数价)`、`pxMark(成交时刻标记价)`——没有就 `null`，但强烈建议你在调用时顺手拉一条 index/mark 的快照  
6) **bills**：现在是空，后续要从 OKX 账变接口拉入（这是强平/ADL 最硬证据）

# 4) “强平 / 不强平”判定（当前数据下的最小规则）
先落**可运行规则**，等你把 OKX 账变 `type/subType` 码表贴给我，再进阶：

优先级从高到低：
1) **账变直证**（需要补 `bills.json`）：  
   - 若出现任一账变记录能明确指示 **强平/ADL** →  
     `status = "liquidated" | "adl"`, `signals += ["BILLS_TYPE_<code>"]`, `reason = "LIQUIDATION"|"ADL"`
2) **仓位归零与被动平仓迹象**（你现在的单暂时无法判断）：  
   - 在短窗口内净持仓从非零→零，且无用户主动平仓信号 → `status = "liquidated"`  
   - 发现 `reduceOnly=true` 的反向成交闭合 → `status = "not_liquidated"`
3) **证据不足**： → `status = "unknown"`（当前这条单就属于此类）

---

> - 所有价格/数量/费率/盈亏都**转为字符串**；时间给原样和 ISO 两份。  
> - `reduceOnly/tdMode` 暂缺就置 `null`。  
> - `tradeId` 是负号也**按字符串**原样入库，不做数值运算。

# 2) 证据叶（建议顺序）
```
query → raw.okx.order → raw.okx.fills → normalized.order → normalized.fills
→ normalized.liquidation → decision
```
用 `keccak256(canonical_json)` 计算叶哈希；得到 `evidence.root` 用于“证据上链预览”。

# 3) 这条单的快速一致性校验
- 成交额校验：`116495.1 × 0.64 = 74556.864` → 你给的 `74556.86` ✅  
- 盈亏/手续费与你提供一致 ✅  
- 方向 `sell` + `posSide: long` → 关闭多仓/被动平仓的典型形态（合理） ✅

# 4) 想把“liquidated”从**推断**升级为**硬证据**，补这两件事
1) **拉 bills**：把 OKX 账变写入 `normalized.bills[]`（原样字段 + 我之前给的映射）。  
   - 若出现强平/ADL 的 `type/subType` 码值：  
     - `liquidation.status = "liquidated" | "adl"`  
     - `liquidation.reason = "LIQUIDATION" | "ADL"`  
     - `signals += ["BILLS_TYPE_<code>"]`
2) **仓位快照（可选）**：短窗内净持仓非零→零，且无 reduce-only 痕迹 → `signals += ["POSITION_ZERO_SPIKE"]`

# 5) 马上可做的小修正
- 把 `order_info.平均成交价格` 也填到 `normalized.order.avgPx`（已做）。  
- 前端展示：`normalized.order / fills` + `liquidation.status` + `evidence.root` + “复制 root”。  
- `/api/attest/preview` 入参只要 `verifyId`，回出 `{ordId, instId, root, timestamp, typedDataV4}`（按之前模板）。

stop ,  结论：下面给出**“账号验证”**与**“理赔（验证→提交→上链记录）”**的全部字段。含：前端表单、后端请求/响应、必要枚举、必填/可选、类型约束、示例与 JSON Schema。按此即可落地。

# 1) 账号验证（Exchange API 验证）

## 1.1 前端表单字段

* `exchange` *(enum, 必填)*：`"OKX" | "Hyperliquid" | "Binance"`
* `label` *(string, 选填)*：账号备注
* `environment` *(enum, 必填)*：`"live" | "testnet"`
* `credentials` *(object, 必填)*：

  * **OKX**：`apiKey` *(string)*，`apiSecret` *(string)*，`passphrase` *(string)*
  * **Hyperliquid**：`apiKey` *(string)*，`apiSecret` *(string)* 或 `signingKey` *(string)*，`accountId?` *(string)*，`subAccount?` *(string)*
* `ipWhitelist?` *(string, 选填)*：提示用，不入库
* 表单规则：验证成功后隐藏敏感输入；失败保留并标红原因

## 1.2 后端创建与验证接口

### POST `/exchange-apis`（创建账号）

请求体

```json
{
  "exchange": "OKX",
  "label": "OKX 主",
  "environment": "live",
  "credentials": { "apiKey":"...", "apiSecret":"...", "passphrase":"..." }
}
```

响应（卡片最小集）

```json
{
  "id":"eacc_123","exchange":"OKX","label":"OKX 主",
  "environment":"live","status":"unverified",
  "masked":{"apiKeyLast4":"a9f2"},
  "createdAt":"2025-10-27T00:00:00Z"
}
```

### POST `/exchange-apis/:id/verify`（触发验证）

请求体

```json
{ "fresh": true, "timeoutMs": 12000 }
```

响应（统一能力检查）

```json
{
  "status":"verified",
  "caps":{"orders":true,"fills":true,"positions":true,"liquidations":true},
  "account":{"exchangeUid":"12345678","subAccount":"main","accountType":"futures","sampleInstruments":["BTC-USDT-PERP"]},
  "proof":{"echo":{"firstOrderIdLast4":"8a3f","firstFillQty":"0.001","firstFillTime":"2025-10-26T12:34:56Z"},"hash":"0xproofhash"},
  "latencyMs": 480,
  "verifiedAt":"2025-10-27T00:10:00Z",
  "reasons":[]
}
```

失败示例

```json
{
  "status":"failed",
  "caps":{"orders":false,"fills":false,"positions":false,"liquidations":false},
  "reasons":["INVALID_CREDENTIALS","IP_NOT_WHITELISTED"]
}
```

## 1.3 JP 验证服务对接（内部）

* `POST /verify/:exchange/account`
  请求

```json
{
  "environment":"live",
  "credentials":{"apiKey":"...","apiSecret":"...","passphrase":"..."}
}
```

响应与 `/exchange-apis/:id/verify` 对齐（同结构）。

---

# 2) 理赔（验证→提交→上链记录）

## 2.1 发起准备

### POST `/claims/prepare`

请求

```json
{ "orderId":"ord_9f2c" }
```

响应

```json
{
  "claimToken":"ctkn_XYZ",
  "order":{
    "id":"ord_9f2c",
    "coverageStartTs":1730000000,
    "coverageEndTs":1730086400,
    "exchangeAccountId":"eacc_123",
    "payoutMax":60,
    "status":"active"
  },
  "expiresAt":"2025-10-27T03:00:00Z"
}
```

## 2.2 证据验证

### POST `/claims/verify`

请求

```json
{
  "orderId":"ord_9f2c",
  "exchangeAccountId":"eacc_123",
  "orderRef":"2938801601245126656",
  "claimToken":"ctkn_XYZ"
}
```

响应（成功示例）

```json
{
  "eligible": true,
  "payout": 48.5,
  "currency": "USDC",
  "evidence": {
    "type": "LIQUIDATION",            // or "ADL"
    "time": "2025-10-27T02:10:00Z",
    "pair": "BTC-USDT-PERP",
    "echo": { "orderIdLast4":"8a3f","qty":"0.002","price":"67350" },
    "merkleRoot": "0xmerkle..."
  },
  "claimId": "clm_9x1",
  "voucher": {
    "domain": { "name":"LiqPass","version":"1","chainId":8453,"verifyingContract":"0xLeverageGuard" },
    "types": {
      "Claim":[
        {"name":"orderDigest","type":"bytes32"},
        {"name":"claimId","type":"bytes32"},
        {"name":"beneficiary","type":"address"},
        {"name":"amount","type":"uint256"},
        {"name":"evidenceRoot","type":"bytes32"},
        {"name":"expiry","type":"uint64"},
        {"name":"nonce","type":"uint256"}
      ]
    },
    "values": {
      "orderDigest":"0xdead...",
      "claimId":"0x0000...9x1",
      "beneficiary":"0xUserWallet",
      "amount":"48500000",
      "evidenceRoot":"0xmerkle...",
      "expiry":1730080000,
      "nonce":"123"
    },
    "signature":"0xattestorSig"
  },
  "reasons": []
}
```

失败示例

```json
{
  "eligible": false,
  "payout": 0,
  "currency": "USDC",
  "evidence": null,
  "reasons": ["OUT_OF_WINDOW","NO_LIQUIDATION_FOUND"]
}
```

> JP 服务镜像接口：`POST /verify/:exchange/claim`，请求体：

```json
{
  "credentials":{"apiKey":"..."},
  "orderRef":"2938801601245126656",
  "window":{"start":1730000000,"end":1730086400},
  "pair":"BTC-USDT-PERP"
}
```

返回体与上文 `eligible/payout/evidence/reasons` 对齐。

## 2.3 提交理赔

### POST `/claims/submit`

请求

```json
{
  "orderId":"ord_9f2c",
  "claimToken":"ctkn_XYZ",
  "userConfirmedEcho": true
}
```

响应

```json
{ "claimId":"clm_9x1", "status":"in_review", "payout":48.5, "currency":"USDC" }
```

## 2.4 上链记录（拉取式赔付）

### 前端链上调用

* 合约函数：`claimPayout(Claim c, bytes attestorSig)`
* 由上一步 `voucher` 直接构造参数，用户付 gas

### POST `/claims/:id/record-onchain`

请求

```json
{ "txHash":"0xpay..." }
```

响应

```json
{ "status":"paid","txHash":"0xpay..." }
```

---

# 3) 字段清单（汇总一览）

## 3.1 验证（账号）

* 输入

  * `exchange` *(enum, 必)*：`OKX|Hyperliquid|Binance`
  * `environment` *(enum, 必)*：`live|testnet`
  * `credentials` *(object, 必)*：见各所必填
  * `fresh` *(bool, 选)*，`timeoutMs` *(int, 选)*
* 输出

  * `status` *(enum)*：`verified|failed|partial|error`
  * `caps` *(object<bool>)*：`orders|fills|positions|liquidations`
  * `account` *(object)*：`exchangeUid|subAccount|accountType|sampleInstruments[]`
  * `proof.echo` *(object)*：`firstOrderIdLast4|firstFillQty|firstFillTime`
  * `proof.hash` *(string)*
  * `latencyMs` *(int)*，`verifiedAt` *(ISO)*，`reasons[]` *(string[])*

## 3.2 理赔（验证→提交→上链）

* 准备 `/claims/prepare`：

  * 入：`orderId`
  * 出：`claimToken`，`order{coverageStartTs,coverageEndTs,exchangeAccountId,payoutMax,status}`
* 验证 `/claims/verify`：

  * 入：`orderId`，`exchangeAccountId`，`orderRef?`，`claimToken`
  * 出：`eligible`，`payout`，`currency`，`evidence{type,time,pair,echo{orderIdLast4,qty,price},merkleRoot}`，`claimId`，`voucher{domain,types,values,signature}`，`reasons[]`
* 提交 `/claims/submit`：

  * 入：`orderId`，`claimToken`，`userConfirmedEcho`
  * 出：`claimId`，`status`
* 上链记录 `/claims/:id/record-onchain`：

  * 入：`txHash`
  * 出：`status`，`txHash`

---

# 4) JSON Schema（给 AI 直接用）

### `/exchange-apis/:id/verify` 响应

```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["status","caps"],
  "properties":{
    "status":{"type":"string","enum":["verified","failed","partial","error"]},
    "caps":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "orders":{"type":"boolean"},
      "fills":{"type":"boolean"},
      "positions":{"type":"boolean"},
      "liquidations":{"type":"boolean"}
    }, "required":["orders","fills","positions","liquidations"]},
    "account":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "exchangeUid":{"type":"string"},
      "subAccount":{"type":"string"},
      "accountType":{"type":"string"},
      "sampleInstruments":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}
    }},
    "proof":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "echo":{"type":"object","properties":{
        "firstOrderIdLast4":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[0-9a-fA-F]{4}$"},
        "firstFillQty":{"type":"string"},
        "firstFillTime":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"}
      }, "required":["firstOrderIdLast4"]},
      "hash":{"type":"string"}
    }},
    "latencyMs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0},
    "verifiedAt":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"},
    "reasons":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}
  }
}
```

### `/claims/verify` 请求

```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["orderId","exchangeAccountId","claimToken"],
  "properties":{
    "orderId":{"type":"string"},
    "exchangeAccountId":{"type":"string"},
    "orderRef":{"type":"string"},
    "claimToken":{"type":"string"}
  }
}
```

### `/claims/verify` 响应

```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["eligible","payout","currency","claimId"],
  "properties":{
    "eligible":{"type":"boolean"},
    "payout":{"type":"number","minimum":0},
    "currency":{"type":"string","enum":["USDC"]},
    "evidence":{"type":"object","nullable":true,"properties":{
      "type":{"type":"string","enum":["LIQUIDATION","ADL"]},
      "time":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"},
      "pair":{"type":"string"},
      "echo":{"type":"object","properties":{
        "orderIdLast4":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[0-9a-fA-F]{4}$"},
        "qty":{"type":"string"},
        "price":{"type":"string"}
      }},
      "merkleRoot":{"type":"string"}
    }},
    "claimId":{"type":"string"},
    "voucher":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "domain":{"type":"object"},
      "types":{"type":"object"},
      "values":{"type":"object"},
      "signature":{"type":"string"}
    }},
    "reasons":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}
  }
}
```

### `/claims/submit` 请求

```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["orderId","claimToken","userConfirmedEcho"],
  "properties":{
    "orderId":{"type":"string"},
    "claimToken":{"type":"string"},
    "userConfirmedEcho":{"type":"boolean"}
  }
}
```

# 3) 枚举 & 约束（统一表）
- `Exchange`：`OKX | Hyperliquid | Binance`
- `Environment`：`live | testnet`
- `/exchange-apis/:id/verify status`：`verified | failed | partial | error`
- `caps`：`orders | fills | positions | liquidations`（bool）
- `Evidence.type`：`LIQUIDATION | ADL`
- 失败原因 `reasons[]`（建议取值）：  
  `INVALID_CREDENTIALS, IP_NOT_WHITELISTED, PERMISSION_DENIED, RATE_LIMIT, EXCHANGE_DOWN, NETWORK_ERROR, UNSUPPORTED_SYMBOL, OUT_OF_WINDOW, NO_LIQUIDATION_FOUND, ORDER_NOT_COVERED, POLICY_EXPIRED, DUP_CLAIM, BAD_VOUCHER, SIGNATURE_MISMATCH`
- 金额/数量：接口展示可用 `number`；EIP-712 `values.amount` 等**必须字符串**（uint/最小单位）

# 4) JSON Schema（可直接校验）

### 4.1 POST `/exchange-apis` 请求
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["exchange","environment","credentials"],
  "properties":{
    "exchange":{"type":"string","enum":["OKX","Hyperliquid","Binance"]},
    "label":{"type":"string","maxLength":64},
    "environment":{"type":"string","enum":["live","testnet"]},
    "credentials":{
      "type":"object",
      "unevaluatedProperties":false,
      "oneOf":[
        {
          "properties":{"apiKey":{"type":"string"},"apiSecret":{"type":"string"},"passphrase":{"type":"string"}},
          "required":["apiKey","apiSecret","passphrase"],
          "title":"OKX"
        },
        {
          "properties":{"apiKey":{"type":"string"},"apiSecret":{"type":"string"},"signingKey":{"type":"string"},"accountId":{"type":"string"},"subAccount":{"type":"string"}},
          "required":["apiKey"],
          "anyOf":[{"required":["apiSecret"]},{"required":["signingKey"]}],
          "title":"Hyperliquid"
        },
        {
          "properties":{"apiKey":{"type":"string"},"apiSecret":{"type":"string"}},
          "required":["apiKey","apiSecret"],
          "title":"Binance"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

### 4.2 POST `/exchange-apis/:id/verify` 请求
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "properties":{"fresh":{"type":"boolean"},"timeoutMs":{"type":"integer","minimum":1000,"maximum":60000}}
}
```

### 4.3 POST `/exchange-apis/:id/verify` 响应
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["status","caps"],
  "properties":{
    "status":{"type":"string","enum":["verified","failed","partial","error"]},
    "caps":{"type":"object","required":["orders","fills","positions","liquidations"],
      "properties":{"orders":{"type":"boolean"},"fills":{"type":"boolean"},"positions":{"type":"boolean"},"liquidations":{"type":"boolean"}}},
    "account":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "exchangeUid":{"type":"string"},"subAccount":{"type":"string"},"accountType":{"type":"string"},
      "sampleInstruments":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}
    }},
    "proof":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "echo":{"type":"object","properties":{
        "firstOrderIdLast4":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[0-9a-fA-F]{4}$"},
        "firstFillQty":{"type":"string"},
        "firstFillTime":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"}
      }},
      "hash":{"type":"string"}
    }},
    "latencyMs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0},
    "verifiedAt":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"},
    "reasons":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}
  }
}
```

### 4.4 POST `/claims/prepare` 请求 & 响应
**请求**
```json
{ "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema","type":"object","required":["orderId"],
  "properties":{"orderId":{"type":"string"}}}
```
**响应**
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["claimToken","order","expiresAt"],
  "properties":{
    "claimToken":{"type":"string"},
    "order":{"type":"object","required":["id","coverageStartTs","coverageEndTs","exchangeAccountId","payoutMax","status"],
      "properties":{
        "id":{"type":"string"},
        "coverageStartTs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0},
        "coverageEndTs":{"type":"integer","minimum":0},
        "exchangeAccountId":{"type":"string"},
        "payoutMax":{"type":"number","minimum":0},
        "status":{"type":"string","enum":["active","expired","cancelled","claimed"]}
      }},
    "expiresAt":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"}
  }
}
```

### 4.5 POST `/claims/verify` 请求
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["orderId","exchangeAccountId","claimToken"],
  "properties":{
    "orderId":{"type":"string"},
    "exchangeAccountId":{"type":"string"},
    "orderRef":{"type":"string"},
    "claimToken":{"type":"string"}
  }
}
```

### 4.6 POST `/claims/verify` 响应
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["eligible","payout","currency","claimId"],
  "properties":{
    "eligible":{"type":"boolean"},
    "payout":{"type":"number","minimum":0},
    "currency":{"type":"string","enum":["USDC"]},
    "evidence":{"oneOf":[
      {"type":"null"},
      {"type":"object","required":["type","time","pair","merkleRoot"],
       "properties":{
         "type":{"type":"string","enum":["LIQUIDATION","ADL"]},
         "time":{"type":"string","format":"date-time"},
         "pair":{"type":"string"},
         "echo":{"type":"object","properties":{
           "orderIdLast4":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[0-9a-fA-F]{4}$"},
           "qty":{"type":"string"},
           "price":{"type":"string"}
         }},
         "merkleRoot":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$"}
       }}
    ]},
    "claimId":{"type":"string"},
    "voucher":{"type":"object","properties":{
      "domain":{"type":"object","required":["name","version","chainId","verifyingContract"],
        "properties":{"name":{"type":"string"},"version":{"type":"string"},"chainId":{"type":"integer"},"verifyingContract":{"type":"string"}}},
      "types":{"type":"object"},
      "values":{"type":"object","required":["orderDigest","claimId","beneficiary","amount","evidenceRoot","expiry","nonce"],
        "properties":{
          "orderDigest":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$"},
          "claimId":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$"},
          "beneficiary":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{40}$"},
          "amount":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[0-9]+$"},
          "evidenceRoot":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$"},
          "expiry":{"type":"integer","minimum":0},
          "nonce":{"type":"string","pattern":"^[0-9]+$"}
        }},
      "signature":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]+$"}
    }},
    "reasons":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string"}}
  }
}
```

### 4.7 POST `/claims/submit` 请求 & 响应
**请求**
```json
{
  "$schema":"https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "type":"object",
  "required":["orderId","claimToken","userConfirmedEcho"],
  "properties":{
    "orderId":{"type":"string"},
    "claimToken":{"type":"string"},
    "userConfirmedEcho":{"type":"boolean"}
  }
}
```

**响应**

```json
    "status":{"type":"string","enum":["in_review","rejected","approved","paid"]},
    "payout":{"type":"number"},
    "currency":{"type":"string","enum":["USDC"]}
  }
}
```

### 4.8 POST `/claims/:id/record-onchain` 请求 & 响应

```json
}
```

```json
  "type":"object",

  "properties":{
    "status":{"type":"string","enum":["pending","paid","failed"]},
    "txHash":{"type":"string","pattern":"^0x[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$"}
  }
}
```

# 1) 账号验证（Exchange API 验证）

## 1.1 前端表单字段
- `exchange` *(enum, 必填)*：`"OKX" | "Hyperliquid" | "Binance"`  
- `label` *(string, 选填)*：账号备注  
- `environment` *(enum, 必填)*：`"live" | "testnet"`  
- `credentials` *(object, 必填)*：
  - **OKX**：`apiKey` *(string)*，`apiSecret` *(string)*，`passphrase` *(string)*
  - **Hyperliquid**：`apiKey` *(string)*，`apiSecret` *(string)* 或 `signingKey` *(string)*，`accountId?` *(string)*，`subAccount?` *(string)*
- `ipWhitelist?` *(string, 选填)*：提示用，不入库  
- 表单规则：验证成功后隐藏敏感输入；失败保留并标红原因

## 1.2 后端创建与验证接口

### POST `/exchange-apis`（创建账号）
请求体
```json
{
  "exchange": "OKX",
  "label": "OKX 主",
  "environment": "live",
  "credentials": { "apiKey":"...", "apiSecret":"...", "passphrase":"..." }
}
```
响应（卡片最小集）
```json
{
  "id":"eacc_123","exchange":"OKX","label":"OKX 主",
  "environment":"live","status":"unverified",
  "masked":{"apiKeyLast4":"a9f2"},
  "createdAt":"2025-10-27T00:00:00Z"
}
```

### POST `/exchange-apis/:id/verify`（触发验证）
请求体
```json
{ "fresh": true, "timeoutMs": 12000 }
```
响应（统一能力检查）
```json
{
  "status":"verified",
  "caps":{"orders":true,"fills":true,"positions":true,"liquidations":true},
  "account":{"exchangeUid":"12345678","subAccount":"main","accountType":"futures","sampleInstruments":["BTC-USDT-PERP"]},
  "proof":{"echo":{"firstOrderIdLast4":"8a3f","firstFillQty":"0.001","firstFillTime":"2025-10-26T12:34:56Z"},"hash":"0xproofhash"},
  "latencyMs": 480,
  "verifiedAt":"2025-10-27T00:10:00Z",
  "reasons":[]
}
```
失败示例
```json
{
  "status":"failed",
  "caps":{"orders":false,"fills":false,"positions":false,"liquidations":false},
  "reasons":["INVALID_CREDENTIALS","IP_NOT_WHITELISTED"]
}
```

## 1.3 JP 验证服务对接（内部）
- `POST /verify/:exchange/account`
请求
```json
{
  "environment":"live",
  "credentials":{"apiKey":"...","apiSecret":"...","passphrase":"..."}
}
```
响应与 `/exchange-apis/:id/verify` 对齐（同结构）。


```json
  "claimToken":"ctkn_XYZ",

  },
  "expiresAt":"2025-10-27T03:00:00Z"
}
```

### POST `/claims/verify`
请求
```json
{
  "orderId":"ord_9f2c",
  "exchangeAccountId":"eacc_123",
  "orderRef":"2938801601245126656",
  "claimToken":"ctkn_XYZ"
}
```
响应（成功示例）
```json
{
  "eligible": true,
  "payout": 48.5,
  "currency": "USDC",
  "evidence": {
    "type": "LIQUIDATION",            // or "ADL"
    "time": "2025-10-27T02:10:00Z",
    "pair": "BTC-USDT-PERP",
    "echo": { "orderIdLast4":"8a3f","qty":"0.002","price":"67350" },
    "merkleRoot": "0xmerkle..."
  },
  "claimId": "clm_9x1",
  "voucher": {
    "domain": { "name":"LiqPass","version":"1","chainId":8453,"verifyingContract":"0xLeverageGuard" },
    "types": {
      "Claim":[
        {"name":"orderDigest","type":"bytes32"},
        {"name":"claimId","type":"bytes32"},
        {"name":"beneficiary","type":"address"},
        {"name":"amount","type":"uint256"},
        {"name":"evidenceRoot","type":"bytes32"},
        {"name":"expiry","type":"uint64"},
        {"name":"nonce","type":"uint256"}
      ]
    },
    "values": {
      "orderDigest":"0xdead...",
      "claimId":"0x0000...9x1",
      "beneficiary":"0xUserWallet",
      "amount":"48500000",
      "evidenceRoot":"0xmerkle...",
      "expiry":1730080000,
      "nonce":"123"
    },
    "signature":"0xattestorSig"
  },
  "reasons": []
}
```
失败示例
```json
{
  "eligible": false,
  "payout": 0,
  "currency": "USDC",
  "evidence": null,
  "reasons": ["OUT_OF_WINDOW","NO_LIQUIDATION_FOUND"]
}
```

> JP 服务镜像接口：`POST /verify/:exchange/claim`，请求体：
```json
{
  "credentials":{"apiKey":"..."},
  "orderRef":"2938801601245126656",
  "window":{"start":1730000000,"end":1730086400},
  "pair":"BTC-USDT-PERP"
}
```
返回体与上文 `eligible/payout/evidence/reasons` 对齐。

## 2.3 提交理赔

### POST `/claims/submit`
请求
```json
{
  "orderId":"ord_9f2c",
  "claimToken":"ctkn_XYZ",
  "userConfirmedEcho": true
}
```
响应
```json
{ "claimId":"clm_9x1", "status":"in_review", "payout":48.5, "currency":"USDC" }
```

## 2.4 上链记录（拉取式赔付）

### 前端链上调用
- 合约函数：`claimPayout(Claim c, bytes attestorSig)`
- 由上一步 `voucher` 直接构造参数，用户付 gas

### POST `/claims/:id/record-onchain`
请求
```json
{ "txHash":"0xpay..." }
```
响应
```json
{ "status":"paid","txHash":"0xpay..." }
```

# 3) 字段清单（汇总一览）

## 3.1 验证（账号）
- 输入
  - `exchange` *(enum, 必)*：`OKX|Hyperliquid|Binance`
  - `environment` *(enum, 必)*：`live|testnet`
  - `credentials` *(object, 必)*：见各所必填
  - `fresh` *(bool, 选)*，`timeoutMs` *(int, 选)*
- 输出
  - `status` *(enum)*：`verified|failed|partial|error`
  - `caps` *(object<bool>)*：`orders|fills|positions|liquidations`
  - `account` *(object)*：`exchangeUid|subAccount|accountType|sampleInstruments[]`
  - `proof.echo` *(object)*：`firstOrderIdLast4|firstFillQty|firstFillTime`
  - `proof.hash` *(string)*  
  - `latencyMs` *(int)*，`verifiedAt` *(ISO)*，`reasons[]` *(string[])*

## 3.2 理赔（验证→提交→上链）
- 准备 `/claims/prepare`：
  - 入：`orderId`
  - 出：`claimToken`，`order{coverageStartTs,coverageEndTs,exchangeAccountId,payoutMax,status}`
- 验证 `/claims/verify`：
  - 入：`orderId`，`exchangeAccountId`，`orderRef?`，`claimToken`
  - 出：`eligible`，`payout`，`currency`，`evidence{type,time,pair,echo{orderIdLast4,qty,price},merkleRoot}`，`claimId`，`voucher{domain,types,values,signature}`，`reasons[]`
- 提交 `/claims/submit`：
  - 入：`orderId`，`claimToken`，`userConfirmedEcho`  
  - 出：`claimId`，`status`
- 上链记录 `/claims/:id/record-onchain`：
  - 入：`txHash`
  - 出：`status`，`txHash`

# 一、新用户【验证交易所订单】（前端→JP-verify）

## 0. 前置：保存/校验只读密钥
- `POST /auth/ping`（可选）
  - **请求字段**
    - `exchange`: `"okx" | "binance"`
    - （OKX）`apiKey`, `secretKey`, `passphrase`, `uid`
    - （Binance）`apiKey`, `secretKey`
  - **响应字段**：`ok: boolean`, `reason?: string`

> 若不落库，密钥只在本次会话随请求传给 JP-verify 即可。

## 1. 发起订单验证
- **URL**：`POST http://<JP_IP>:8082/api/verify`
- **请求字段（统一层）**
  - `exchange`: `"okx" | "binance"`
  - `live`: `boolean`（直连交易所）
  - `fresh`: `boolean`（强制绕过缓存）
  - `noCache`: `boolean`
  - `includeRaw`: `boolean`（返回原始响应）
  - `includePerf`: `boolean`
  - `clientIp?`: `string`（可做风控）
- **请求字段（交易所差异层）**
  - **OKX**
    - `ordId`: `string`（订单号，优先）
    - `instId`: `string`（如 `BTC-USDT-SWAP`）
    - `apiKey`, `secretKey`, `passphrase`, `uid`: `string`
    - 可选：`clOrdId?: string`, `posSide?: "long"|"short"`, `mgnMode?: "cross"|"isolated"`
  - **Binance（U本位合约）**
    - `symbol`: `string`（如 `BTCUSDT`）
    - `orderId?: number` 或 `origClientOrderId?: string`（二选一）
    - `apiKey`, `secretKey`: `string`
    - 可选：`positionSide?: "BOTH"|"LONG"|"SHORT"`, `recvWindow?: number`

- **响应字段（`VerifyResult` 统一结构）**
  - `status`: `"ok" | "error"`
  - `error?`: `{code: string, message: string}`
  - `meta`:  
    - `exchange`, `instIdOrSymbol`, `orderId`, `clientOrderId?`
    - `accountIdHash`, `userIdHash`
    - `requestTs`, `source: "live"|"cache"`
  - `normalized`（**跨交易所对齐后的关键字段**）
    - `order`:  
      - `type`: `"market"|"limit"|...`
      - `side`: `"buy"|"sell"`
      - `positionSide?`: `"long"|"short"|"BOTH"`
      - `status`: `"new"|"partiallyFilled"|"filled"|"canceled"|"rejected"`
      - `leverage?`: `string|number`
      - `qty`: `string`（基础数量）
      - `avgFillPrice?`: `string`
      - `fee?`: `{asset: string, amount: string}`
      - `reduceOnly?`: `boolean`
      - `createdAt`: `number`（ms）
      - `updatedAt?`: `number`（ms）
    - `position?`:  
      - `size`, `entryPrice`, `liqPx?`, `marginMode`, `unrealizedPnL?`
    - `marketSnapshot`: `{ markPrice: string, indexPrice?: string, ts: number }`
  - `raw?`: `{ order?: any, fills?: any[], position?: any, ticker?: any }`
  - `evidence`:  
    - `orderIdHash`: `string`（keccak256）
    - `leaves`: `Array<{k:string,v:string|number|boolean, ts:number}>`
    - `root`: `string`（Merkle root）
    - `parentRoot?`: `string`（可选父根，用于审计链）
  - `perf?`: `{tTotalMs:number, tExchMs:number, tAssembleMs:number}`
  - `sig`: `{digest:string, signature:string, signer:string, expireAt:number}`
  - `cache`: `{key:string, fresh:boolean}`

- **OKX 示例请求**
```json
{
  "exchange": "okx",
  "live": true,
  "fresh": true,
  "noCache": true,
  "includeRaw": true,
  "includePerf": true,
  "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
  "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "apiKey": "****",
  "secretKey": "****",
  "passphrase": "****",
  "uid": "****"
}
```

- **Binance 示例请求**
```json
{
  "exchange": "binance",
  "live": true,
  "fresh": true,
  "noCache": true,
  "includeRaw": true,
  "symbol": "BTCUSDT",
  "orderId": 1234567890,
  "apiKey": "****",
  "secretKey": "****"
}
```

---

# 二、【爆仓后 → 验证 → 理赔 → 上链】（端到端）

## 概览步骤
1) **用户点击“验证爆仓”** → `POST /api/verify`（拿到 `VerifyResult`）  
2) **风控判定是否符合赔付规则** → 生成 `ClaimDecision`  
3) **服务器签发理赔凭证 Voucher** + **上链登记证据根**  
4) **用户链上 `claimPayout`** → 合约发 USDC/记录事件

## 1. 风控判定（Server 内部）
- 输入：`VerifyResult`、`policy`、规则引擎参数
- **规则参数字段（示例）**
  - `coverageWindow`: `{start:number, end:number}`（ms）
  - `leverageRange`: `{min:number, max:number}`
  - `principalCap`: `string`（USDC）
  - `skuId`: `"24h"|"8hWindow"|"monthlyDrawdown"|"noLiqCashback"`
  - `waitingPeriodMs`: `number`
  - `blacklist`: `string[]`（address 或 accountIdHash）
  - `maxClaimsPerPolicy`: `number`
- **判定输出 `ClaimDecision`**
  - `eligible`: `boolean`
  - `reason?`: `string`
  - `payoutAmount?`: `string`
  - `policyId`: `string`
  - `orderIdHash`: `string`
  - `evidenceRoot`: `string`
  - `proof`: `string[]`（Merkle 分支）
  - `decidedAt`: `number`

## 2. 签发理赔凭证（EIP-712 Voucher）
- **后端出具 `/claims/voucher`**
  - **请求字段**
    - `policyId`: `string`
    - `orderIdHash`: `string`
  - **响应字段 `ClaimVoucher`**
    - `claimId`: `string`
    - `policyId`: `string`
    - `claimant`: `string`（用户链上地址）
    - `payoutAmount`: `string`（USDC，最小单位或6位小数）
    - `evidenceRoot`: `bytes32`
    - `orderIdHash`: `bytes32`
    - `proofHash`: `bytes32`（对 `proof[]` 做 keccak256）
    - `nonce`: `uint256`
    - `deadline`: `uint256`（unix 秒）
    - `chainId`: `uint256`
    - `verifyingContract`: `address`
    - `signature`: `bytes`（**服务器私钥对下述 EIP-712 message 的签名**）

- **EIP-712 TypedData（建议）**
  - `domain`:  
    - `name`: `"LiqPass"`
    - `version`: `"1"`
    - `chainId`: `uint256`
    - `verifyingContract`: `address`（`ClaimPayout` 合约）
  - `types.Claim`:  
    - `claimId: bytes32`
    - `policyId: bytes32`
    - `claimant: address`
    - `payoutAmount: uint256`
    - `evidenceRoot: bytes32`
    - `orderIdHash: bytes32`
    - `proofHash: bytes32`
    - `nonce: uint256`
    - `deadline: uint256`
  - `message`: 上述同名字段

## 3. 上链登记证据根（Attest）
- **合约方法（示例）**：`attest(bytes32 root, bytes32 parentRoot, bytes32 orderIdHash, uint64 issuedAt)`
  - **输入字段**
    - `root`, `parentRoot?`, `orderIdHash`, `issuedAt`
  - **事件**
    - `Attested(bytes32 root, bytes32 parentRoot, bytes32 orderIdHash, uint64 issuedAt)`

> 可在签发 Voucher 同时由后端直接调用一次 `attest`，保证链上可查。

## 4. 用户链上理赔（ClaimPayout）
- **合约方法（示例）**：  
  `claimPayout(Claim calldata c, bytes calldata serverSig, bytes32[] calldata proof)`
  - **结构 `Claim` 对应上面的 EIP-712 `message`**
  - **输入字段**
    - `c.claimId, c.policyId, c.claimant, c.payoutAmount, c.evidenceRoot, c.orderIdHash, c.proofHash, c.nonce, c.deadline`
    - `serverSig`: 后端签名
    - `proof`: Merkle `leaf -> root` 路径
  - **校验逻辑（合约）**
    1. `block.timestamp <= deadline`
    2. `!usedNonce[claimant][nonce]`
    3. `EIP712.recover(serverSig) == trustedSigner`
    4. `Merkle.verify(proof, c.evidenceRoot, leaf)`（leaf 建议 `keccak256(orderIdHash || payoutAmount || claimant)`）
    5. `attestedRoots[c.evidenceRoot] == true`
  - **成功事件**
    - `Payout(claimId, claimant, payoutAmount, evidenceRoot, orderIdHash)`

---

# 三、购买保单（Policy）字段（为理赔前置）
> 你之前的 SKU/系数模型一起固化，便于理赔核算。

- **`buyPolicy` 请求/落库字段**
  - `policyId`: `string`
  - `buyer`: `address`
  - `skuId`: `"24h"|"8hWindow"|"monthlyDrawdown"|"noLiqCashback"`
  - `principal`: `string`（USDC）
  - `leverage`: `number`
  - `payoutMode`: `"fixed"|"ratio"`
  - `payoutFixed?`: `string`（USDC）
  - `payoutRate?`: `string`（如 `"0.6"` 表示 60%）
  - `premiumPaid`: `string`（USDC）
  - `riskCoeff`: `string`（**系数 α，默认 `"1.0"`，动态调节**）
  - `coverageWindow`: `{start:number, end:number}`（ms）
  - `termsVersion`: `string`
  - `chainId`, `usdc`, `txHash`
  - `createdAt`: `number`

---

# 四、服务端落库表（最小可用字段）

- **`users`**
  - `userId`, `address`, `createdAt`, `status`

- **`api_keys`**（若落库，务必加密）
  - `userId`, `exchange`, `apiKeyEnc`, `secretKeyEnc`, `passphraseEnc?`, `uidEnc?`, `createdAt`, `lastCheckedAt`, `scope: "readonly"`

- **`verify_logs`**
  - `id`, `userId`, `exchange`, `orderId`, `instIdOrSymbol`, `orderIdHash`, `evidenceRoot`, `source`, `requestTs`, `resultJsonPath`（指向离线 JSON）

- **`evidence_blobs`**
  - `root`, `parentRoot?`, `orderIdHash`, `leavesCount`, `blobPath`, `issuedAt`

- **`policies`**
  - 上述 `buyPolicy` 全部

- **`claims`**
  - `claimId`, `policyId`, `userId`, `orderIdHash`, `evidenceRoot`, `proofHash`, `payoutAmount`, `status: "prepared"|"signed"|"paid"|"rejected"`, `reason?`, `nonce`, `deadline`, `createdAt`, `txHash?`

- **`attested_roots`**
  - `root`, `parentRoot?`, `orderIdHash`, `attestedAt`, `txHash`

- **`payouts`**
  - `claimId`, `to`, `amount`, `token`, `txHash`, `paidAt`

- **`blacklist`**
  - `subject`（address 或 accountIdHash）, `reason`, `until?`

---

# 五、前端交互最少字段（按钮流）

1) **验证爆仓**
```json
POST /api/verify
{
  "exchange": "okx",
  "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
  "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "apiKey": "****", "secretKey": "****", "passphrase": "****", "uid": "****",
  "live": true, "fresh": true, "noCache": true, "includeRaw": false, "includePerf": true
}
```
前端只用 `status==="ok"` 且 `normalized.position.liqPx` 触发过、或根据 `normalized.order.status`+行情判断强平/ADL。

2) **申请理赔预审**
```json
POST /claims/prepare
{
  "policyId": "POLY_2025_0001",
  "orderIdHash": "0x...",
  "evidenceRoot": "0x..."
}
```
返回 `ClaimDecision`。

3) **领取凭证**
```json
POST /claims/voucher
{
  "policyId": "POLY_2025_0001",
  "orderIdHash": "0x..."
}
```
返回 `ClaimVoucher`（含 `signature`）。

4) **链上理赔**
把 `Claim` 与 `serverSig`、`proof[]` 送入合约 `claimPayout`。

---

# 六、关键校验点（避免争议）

- **订单唯一性**：始终以 `orderIdHash = keccak256(exchange || instIdOrSymbol || ordId || uid?)`
- **证据不可抵赖**：`leaves` 覆盖：订单核心字段、仓位快照、行情快照、服务器时间窗；`root` 上链 `attest`
- **凭证防重放**：`nonce` + `deadline` + `usedNonce` 映射
- **额度一致性**：`payoutAmount` 由 `policy` 与规则计算，入 `leaf`，与合约侧重算结果一致
- **系数 α**：落在 `policy.riskCoeff`，参与 premium 与赔付上限计算（你们的动态调节因子）

---

# 0）本机架构与启动（最少可行）
- 单机组件（都在 `localhost`）  
  1. **Local Verify API**（FastAPI/Node，端口建议 `8082`）直连 OKX/币安只读接口  
  2. **Rules Engine**（进程内即可）输出是否赔付与金额  
  3. **Payout Signer**（本机私钥，签 EIP-712 Voucher）  
  4. **On-chain Client**（web3/ethers，直连 Base/Sepolia/Mainnet）
- `.env` 最少：
  - `SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY`（签发理赔凭证用）
  - `RPC_URL`（链节点）

  - （可选）`STORE_DIR`（离线证据 JSON 存放目录）

# 1）新用户：本机【验证交易所订单】

## 1.1 请求（VerifyRequest）
**通用层**
- `exchange`: `"okx" | "binance"`
- `live`: `boolean`（直连交易所）
- `fresh`: `boolean`（强制不走缓存）
- `noCache`: `boolean`
- `includeRaw`: `boolean`（返回原始响应）
- `includePerf`: `boolean`
- `clientIp?`: `string`

**OKX 专属**
- `ordId`: `string`（优先）
- `instId`: `string`（例 `BTC-USDT-SWAP`）
- `apiKey`, `secretKey`, `passphrase`, `uid`: `string`
- `clOrdId?`: `string`
- `posSide?`: `"long"|"short"`
- `mgnMode?`: `"cross"|"isolated"`

**Binance U本位专属**
- `symbol`: `string`（例 `BTCUSDT`）
- `orderId?`: `number` 或 `origClientOrderId?`: `string`
- `apiKey`, `secretKey`: `string`
- `positionSide?`: `"BOTH"|"LONG"|"SHORT"`
- `recvWindow?`: `number`

> 本机不落库密钥：只在这次请求中随 body 传入；如果要长期保存，请本地加密（如 libsodium/AES-GCM）。

## 1.2 响应（VerifyResult）
- `status`: `"ok" | "error"`
- `error?`: `{ code: string, message: string }`
- `meta`:
  - `exchange`: `string`
  - `instIdOrSymbol`: `string`
  - `orderId`: `string`
  - `clientOrderId?`: `string`
  - `accountIdHash`: `string`（`keccak256(uid or account)`）
  - `userIdHash`: `string`（可等同 accountIdHash）
  - `requestTs`: `number(ms)`
  - `source`: `"live"|"cache"`
- `normalized`（**跨所对齐后的关键字段**）
  - `order`:
    - `type`: `"market"|"limit"|...`
    - `side`: `"buy"|"sell"`
    - `positionSide?`: `"long"|"short"|"BOTH"`
    - `status`: `"new"|"partiallyFilled"|"filled"|"canceled"|"rejected"`
    - `leverage?`: `string|number`
    - `qty`: `string`
    - `avgFillPrice?`: `string`
    - `fee?`: `{ asset: string, amount: string }`
    - `reduceOnly?`: `boolean`
    - `createdAt`: `number(ms)`
    - `updatedAt?`: `number(ms)`
  - `position?`:
    - `size`: `string`
    - `entryPrice`: `string`
    - `liqPx?`: `string`
    - `marginMode`: `"cross"|"isolated"`
    - `unrealizedPnL?`: `string`
  - `marketSnapshot`:
    - `markPrice`: `string`
    - `indexPrice?`: `string`
    - `ts`: `number(ms)`
- `raw?`: `{ order?: any, fills?: any[], position?: any, ticker?: any }`
- `evidence`：
  - `orderIdHash`: `string`（`keccak256(exchange||instIdOrSymbol||orderId||uid?)`）
  - `leaves`: `Array<{k:string, v:string|number|boolean, ts:number}>`
  - `root`: `string`（Merkle root）
  - `parentRoot?`: `string`
- `perf?`: `{ tTotalMs:number, tExchMs:number, tAssembleMs:number }`
- `sig`: `{ digest:string, signature:string, signer:string, expireAt:number }`
- `cache`: `{ key:string, fresh:boolean }`

**本机 cURL 示例（OKX）**
```bash
curl -sS -m 20 -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
-d '{
  "exchange":"okx","live":true,"fresh":true,"noCache":true,
  "includeRaw":true,"includePerf":true,
  "ordId":"2940071038556348417","instId":"BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "apiKey":"****","secretKey":"****","passphrase":"****","uid":"****"
}' http://127.0.0.1:8082/verify | jq .
```

**本机 cURL 示例（Binance）**
```bash
curl -sS -m 20 -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
-d '{
  "exchange":"binance","live":true,"fresh":true,"noCache":true,
  "includeRaw":true,"symbol":"BTCUSDT","orderId":1234567890,
  "apiKey":"****","secretKey":"****"
}' http://127.0.0.1:8082/verify | jq .
```

# 2）爆仓后：本机【点击验证 → 理赔 → 上链】

## 2.1 风控判定（本机进程内）
**输入**：`VerifyResult` + 本地 `policy` + 规则参数  
**规则字段（最少）**
- `coverageWindow`: `{ start:number(ms), end:number(ms) }`
- `leverageRange`: `{ min:number, max:number }`
- `principalCap`: `string`（USDC）
- `skuId`: `"24h"|"8hWindow"|"monthlyDrawdown"|"noLiqCashback"`
- `waitingPeriodMs`: `number`
- `blacklist`: `string[]`（address 或 accountIdHash）
- `maxClaimsPerPolicy`: `number`

**输出 `ClaimDecision`**
- `eligible`: `boolean`
- `reason?`: `string`
- `payoutAmount?`: `string`（USDC）
- `policyId`: `string`
- `orderIdHash`: `string`
- `evidenceRoot`: `string`
- `proof`: `string[]`（Merkle 分支）
- `decidedAt`: `number(ms)`

> 在 UI 上，“点击验证”即可同步完成 2.1：拿 `VerifyResult` → 即时跑规则 → 给出是否赔付与金额。

## 2.2 签发理赔凭证（本机 EIP-712 Voucher）
**接口**：`POST /claims/voucher`（本机）  
**请求**
- `policyId`: `string`
- `orderIdHash`: `string`

**响应 `ClaimVoucher`**
- `claimId`: `string`
- `policyId`: `string`
- `claimant`: `string`（用户链上地址）
- `payoutAmount`: `string`（USDC，最小单位或 6 位小数）
- `evidenceRoot`: `bytes32`
- `orderIdHash`: `bytes32`
- `proofHash`: `bytes32`（对 `proof[]` 做 `keccak256`）
- `nonce`: `uint256`
- `deadline`: `uint256`（unix 秒）
- `chainId`: `uint256`
- `verifyingContract`: `address`
- `signature`: `bytes`（**本机 `SIGNER_PRIVATE_KEY` 对下述 TypedData 的签名**）

**EIP-712 TypedData（本机固定）**
- `domain`:
  - `name`: `"LiqPass"`
  - `version`: `"1"`
  - `chainId`: `CHAIN_ID`
  - `verifyingContract`: `VERIFYING_CONTRACT`
- `types.Claim`:
  - `claimId: bytes32`
  - `policyId: bytes32`
  - `claimant: address`
  - `payoutAmount: uint256`
  - `evidenceRoot: bytes32`
  - `orderIdHash: bytes32`
  - `proofHash: bytes32`
  - `nonce: uint256`
  - `deadline: uint256`
- `message`: 与上同名字段

## 2.3 上链登记证据根（可选但强烈建议）
**本机直接调用合约**：`attest(bytes32 root, bytes32 parentRoot, bytes32 orderIdHash, uint64 issuedAt)`  
**事件**：`Attested(root, parentRoot, orderIdHash, issuedAt)`

## 2.4 用户链上领取赔付
**合约方法**：  
`claimPayout(Claim c, bytes serverSig, bytes32[] proof)`
- `c` 的字段与 **EIP-712 message** 一致
- 校验（合约侧）：
  1. `block.timestamp <= c.deadline`
  2. `!usedNonce[c.claimant][c.nonce]`
  3. `EIP712.recover(serverSig) == trustedSigner`
  4. `Merkle.verify(proof, c.evidenceRoot, leaf)`（建议 `leaf = keccak256(orderIdHash || payoutAmount || claimant)`）
  5. `attestedRoots[c.evidenceRoot] == true`（若开启 2.3）

**成功事件**：`Payout(claimId, claimant, payoutAmount, evidenceRoot, orderIdHash)`

---

# 3）购买保单（为理赔前置）
**`buyPolicy`（本机接口或前端落库）**
- `policyId`: `string`
- `buyer`: `address`
- `skuId`: `"24h"|"8hWindow"|"monthlyDrawdown"|"noLiqCashback"`
- `principal`: `string`（USDC）
- `leverage`: `number`
- `payoutMode`: `"fixed"|"ratio"`
- `payoutFixed?`: `string`
- `payoutRate?`: `string`（例 `"0.6"` 表示 60%）
- `premiumPaid`: `string`（USDC）
- `riskCoeff`: `string`（系数 α，默认 `"1.0"`）
- `coverageWindow`: `{ start:number(ms), end:number(ms) }`
- `termsVersion`: `string`
- `chainId`, `usdc`, `txHash?`
- `createdAt`: `number(ms)`

---

# 4）本机最小落库（SQLite）
- **`verify_logs`**：`id, exchange, orderId, instIdOrSymbol, orderIdHash, evidenceRoot, requestTs, resultJsonPath`
- **`evidence_blobs`**：`root, parentRoot?, orderIdHash, leavesCount, blobPath, issuedAt`
- **`policies`**：见 `buyPolicy`
- **`claims`**：`claimId, policyId, claimant, orderIdHash, evidenceRoot, proofHash, payoutAmount, status, reason?, nonce, deadline, createdAt, txHash?`
- **`attested_roots`**：`root, parentRoot?, orderIdHash, attestedAt, txHash?`
- **`payouts`**：`claimId, to, amount, token, txHash, paidAt`

---

# 5）按钮级链路（前端对接最少字段）

**A. 验证爆仓**
- 调用：`POST /verify`  
- 关键使用字段：`status==="ok"`, `normalized.position.liqPx` 是否触发、`normalized.order.status`、`marketSnapshot.markPrice`  
- 存档：`evidence.root`, `orderIdHash`, `leaves[]`（离线 JSON）

**B. 预审与金额**
- 本机规则引擎输出 `ClaimDecision`

**C. 领取凭证**
- 调用：`POST /claims/voucher`  
- 使用字段：`ClaimVoucher`（含 `signature`）

**D. 上链**
- 先（可选）本机 `attest`  
- 用户钱包调用合约 `claimPayout( c, serverSig, proof[] )`

---

# 6）错误码（最少集）
- `E_AUTH`：交易所鉴权失败（密钥错误/权限非只读）
- `E_NOT_FOUND`：订单不存在或不属于该账户
- `E_RULE_DENY`：规则拒绝（超窗口/杠杆不符/黑名单）
- `E_SIG`：凭证签名非法或过期
- `E_PROOF`：Merkle 证明不匹配
- `E_ONCHAIN`：链上执行失败（余额不足/nonce已用/未 attested）

---

# 7）一口气能做的事
- 你现在就能把以上**请求/响应字段**抄进本机服务；前端只依赖 `/verify → /claims/voucher` 两个接口就能打通“点击验证→领赔付”的闭环。  
- 需要时，我会补一份**单文件本地 FastAPI**脚本（含 OKX/币安直连、标准化、Merkle 生成、EIP-712 签名、`/verify` 与 `/claims/voucher` 两个端点），上来就能跑 `127.0.0.1:8082`。

# 一、总体建议（先本地跑通，保留“JP 验证接口契约”）

* **保持接口不变**：现在就按 `jp-verify:8082` 的契约在本地跑一个服务（同名同端口）。未来把 `BASE_URL` 从 `http://127.0.0.1:8082` 改成 `https://<jp-ip>:8082` 即可切换，无需改前后端逻辑。
* **只做 OKX**：把 OKX 路径打磨扎实（订单查询→归一化→证据包），币安以后再加。
* **不长期存密钥**：本地阶段默认**临时输入**（只进内存）；若需持久化，用**AES-256-GCM**+本机 KMS 文件（或 libsodium）加密到 SQLite。
* **证据优先**：每次验证都产出**可审计证据包**（normalized/raw/merkle/perf），落盘 + SQLite 可检索；后续想上链，直接取 root 即可。

# 三、接口契约（现在就按“JP 验证接口”实现）

### 1) 验证请求（OKX 专用）

`POST /api/verify`（由 jp-verify 暴露；现在本地，未来 JP）

```json
{
  "exchange": "okx",
  "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
  "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
  "live": true,
  "fresh": true,
  "noCache": true,

  "keyMode": "inline",        // "inline" | "alias"
  "apiKey": "…",              // keyMode=inline 时必填
  "secretKey": "…",
  "passphrase": "…",
  "uid": "201933253463154688",// OKX子账户/UID，如无可留空

  "clientMeta": {             // 可选：前端/调用方元信息
    "source": "us-backend",
    "requestId": "uuid-…"
  }
}
```

> 未来若使用密钥别名：`"keyMode":"alias","keyAlias":"okx-main-001"`，其余密钥字段不传。

### 2) 验证响应（**全量返回**）

```json
{
  "meta": {
    "exchange": "okx",
    "instId": "BTC-USDT-SWAP",
    "ordId": "2940071038556348417",
    "verifiedAt": "2025-11-04T09:10:11Z",
    "live": true,
    "fresh": true,
    "requestId": "uuid-…",
    "version": "jp-verify@1.0.0"
  },
  "normalized": {
    "order": {
      "side": "buy",
      "px": "65000.5",
      "sz": "1.0",
      "state": "filled",           // created/partially_filled/filled/canceled/failed
      "avgFillPx": "65010.2",
      "accFillSz": "1.0",
      "fee": "-1.23",
      "ts": "2025-11-03T22:31:45Z"
    },
    "position": {
      "lever": "50",
      "mode": "isolated",          // isolated/cross
      "liqPx": "64010.0",
      "adl": false,
      "liquidated": true,          // ← 你关心的核心字段
      "liquidatedAt": "2025-11-03T22:32:10Z",
      "reason": "forced-liquidation" // forced-liquidation / adl / manual-close / unknown
    }
  },
  "raw": {
    "trade/order": { "code": "0", "data": [/* OKX 原始返回 */], "fetchedAt":"…"},
    "account/positions": { "code": "0", "data": [/* … */], "fetchedAt":"…"},
    "public/instruments": { "code": "0", "data": [/* … */], "fetchedAt":"…"}
  },
  "evidence": {
    "leaves": [
      {"path":"raw.trade/order","hash":"0x…"},
      {"path":"raw.account/positions","hash":"0x…"},
      {"path":"normalized.position","hash":"0x…"}
    ],
    "root": "0xMERKLE_ROOT",
    "rootAlgo": "keccak256-merkle-v1",
    "bundleHash": "0xBUNDLE_HASH",          // 对整个响应去除非确定字段后的 hash
    "evidenceId": "evi_20251104_xxx",       // 便于索引
    "parentRoot": null                      // 以后做“父链”审计时用
  },
  "perf": {
    "okxRttMs": 420,
    "totalMs": 650,
    "cache": false,
    "rateLimit": {"remaining": 98, "resetSec": 1}
  },
  "error": null                              // 出错时 {code,msg,hint}
}
```

# 四、数据库与落盘（SQLite + 文件）

> **本地阶段**建议**最小表集** + **证据文件落盘**，后续 US/JP 拆分仍可复用。

## 1) SQLite 表（`us-backend/db.sqlite`）

* `users(id, created_at)`
* `api_keys(id, user_id, exchange, key_alias, enc_payload, created_at, last_used_at)`
* `orders(id, user_id, exchange, ord_id, inst_id, verified_at, liquidated, reason, evidence_id)`
* `claims(id, user_id, ord_id, status, payout_amount, created_at, decided_at)`
  `status ∈ {pending, approved, rejected, paid}`
* `audit_logs(id, topic, ref_id, created_at, payload_json)`

> `enc_payload` 存 `{apiKey,secretKey,passphrase,uid}` 的**加密 JSON**；本地不需要就别存。

## 2) 证据文件（`reports/evidence/<yyyy-mm-dd>/evi_*.json`）

* 存**响应原文**（去除敏感字段），文件名含 `evidenceId`。
* 同时存一份 `.root` 纯文本只含 `root`，便于以后对账/上链。

# 五、最小闭环流程（今天就能跑）

1. **启动本地 jp-verify**（FastAPI/uvicorn，端口 8082）

   * 路由：`GET /healthz`；`POST /api/verify`（上面契约）。
   * 仅实现 OKX：调用 **订单详情**、**持仓**（或交易/账单）两个端点，得出 `liquidated` 判定。

2. **us-backend 提供薄代理**

   * `POST /verify/okx`：把前端请求转给 `http://127.0.0.1:8082/api/verify`，拿到响应后：

     * 落盘 `reports/evidence/...`
     * 写入 `orders`（提要 + `evidenceId`）
     * 返回前端**同样结构**（或裁剪 `raw`）

3. **us-frontend 做一个“订单验证器”页面**

   * 输入：`apiKey/secret/passphrase/uid`、`instId`、`ordId`
   * 点击【验证】→ 调 `us-backend /verify/okx` → 展示 `normalized.position.liquidated` 等关键字段；提供“查看证据包”按钮。

4. **自测脚本**（本地终端）

```bash
# 1) 健康
curl -s http://127.0.0.1:8082/healthz

# 2) 真实单验证（替换为你的真实 ordId/instId 与密钥）
curl -sS -m 20 -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{
    "exchange":"okx",
    "ordId":"2940071038556348417",
    "instId":"BTC-USDT-SWAP",
    "live":true, "fresh":true, "noCache":true,
    "keyMode":"inline",
    "apiKey":"__OKX_API_KEY__", "secretKey":"__OKX_SECRET__",
    "passphrase":"__OKX_PASS__", "uid":"__OKX_UID__"
  }' http://127.0.0.1:8082/api/verify | jq .
```

# 六、字段清单（你之前反复要“细节”——这里给全）

### A. 输入字段（OKX 验证）

* `ordId` *(string, required)*
* `instId` *(string, required)* 例：`BTC-USDT-SWAP`
* `live`/`fresh`/`noCache` *(bool, optional)*：控制直连所取、绕缓存
* 凭证三件套：`apiKey`、`secretKey`、`passphrase` *(inline 模式必填)*
* `uid` *(string, optional)*：OKX 子账户/UID
* `keyMode` *(string, required)*：`inline` | `alias`
* `keyAlias` *(string, optional)*：`alias` 模式下用
* `clientMeta` *(object, optional)*：`{source, requestId}`

### B. 关键输出字段

* `normalized.position.liquidated` *(bool)*
* `normalized.position.liquidatedAt` *(ISO8601)*
* `normalized.position.reason` ∈ `forced-liquidation | adl | manual-close | unknown`
* `evidence.root` *(0x…)*
* `evidence.bundleHash` *(0x…)*
* `evidence.evidenceId` *(string)*
* `perf.totalMs` *(int)*
* `error`：标准化错误 `{code,msg,hint}`

### C. 失败/错误约定

* `error.code`：`AUTH_ERROR` / `NOT_FOUND` / `RATE_LIMIT` / `REMOTE_4XX` / `REMOTE_5XX` / `TIMEOUT`
* `error.hint` 给出直观修复建议（时间不同步、密钥错、ordId/instId 不匹配等）

---

# 七、迁移路径（US + JP）

**目标**：只改配置，不改代码。

1. **将 `jp-verify` 拷到日本服务器**（保持 8082）

   * `systemd` 启动；`/healthz` 必须 200
   * `ufw`：先全开 8082 做联调；上线后**仅放行美国后端 IP**
   * **鉴权**：给 `jp-verify` 增加 `X-Signature: HMAC_SHA256(body, JP_SHARED_SECRET)`；US 后端配置同密钥

2. **US 后端改 `.env`**

```
JP_VERIFY_BASE_URL=https://<jp-ip-or-domain>:8082
JP_VERIFY_HMAC_SECRET=xxxx
```

3. **灰度**

   * `us-backend` 增加 `?target=local|jp` 切换参数；默认 `jp`，出现异常自动回退 `local`

4. **可观测性**

   * `GET /metrics`（prometheus 文本）至少暴露：请求次数、P95 耗时、错误数、上游 4xx/5xx

---

# 八、为什么先本地、后 JP？

* 你当前的痛点是**能跑通且稳定**。先本地把**接口与证据格式定死**，一键切换到 JP 只涉及“网络与进程稳定性”，而不再改业务与前端。
* 之前你遇到的 SSH/端口/防火墙问题都会拖慢开发节奏。把“复杂度”留到迁移那一刻再处理，且**只处理一类复杂度（运维）**。

```json
  },
  "evidence": {
    "orderIdHash": "0x…",               // keccak256(exchange||instId||ordId||uid?)
    "leaves": [ { "k":"normalized.position", "vHash":"0x…", "ts":1730692330 }, … ],
    "root": "0xMERKLE_ROOT",
    "rootAlgo": "keccak256-merkle-v1",
    "bundleHash": "0xBUNDLE_HASH",      // 去敏后响应整体哈希
    "evidenceId": "evi_20251104_xxx",
    "parentRoot": null
  },
  "perf": { "okxRttMs": 420, "totalMs": 650, "cache": false },
  "error": null
}
```

> 失败统一：`{ "error": { "code": "AUTH_ERROR|NOT_FOUND|TIMEOUT|REMOTE_4XX|REMOTE_5XX|RATE_LIMIT", "message":"…", "hint":"…" } }`

## 1.2 （可选）`POST /claims/prepare`（本地规则判定）
**Request**
```json
{ "policyId":"POLY_2025_0001", "orderIdHash":"0x…", "evidenceRoot":"0x…" }
```
**Response（ClaimDecision）**
```json
{
  "eligible": true,
  "reason": null,
  "payoutAmount": "120.00",
  "policyId": "POLY_2025_0001",
  "orderIdHash": "0x…",
  "evidenceRoot": "0x…",
  "proof": ["0x…","0x…"],
  "decidedAt": 1730692400000
}
```

## 1.3 `POST /claims/voucher`（签发 EIP-712 凭证）
**Request**
```json
{ "policyId":"POLY_2025_0001", "orderIdHash":"0x…" }
```
**Response（ClaimVoucher）**
```json
{
  "claimId":"clm_2025_0001",
  "policyId":"POLY_2025_0001",
  "claimant":"0xUser",
  "payoutAmount":"120000000",          // USDC最小单位或6位小数，与你合约一致
  "evidenceRoot":"0x…",
  "orderIdHash":"0x…",
  "proofHash":"0x…",
  "nonce":"7",
  "deadline":"1730778800",
  "chainId":"8453",
  "verifyingContract":"0xClaimPayout",
  "signature":"0xSIG"                  // 对下列 EIP-712 message 的签名
}
```

**EIP-712 Domain & Types（固定）**
- domain: `{ name:"LiqPass", version:"1", chainId, verifyingContract }`
- type `Claim`：
  ```
  claimId bytes32
  policyId bytes32
  claimant address
  payoutAmount uint256
  evidenceRoot bytes32
  orderIdHash bytes32
  proofHash bytes32
  nonce uint256
  deadline uint256
  ```

---

# 2）数据库（SQLite）——**建表即用**

> 命名规约：`snake_case`；金额类使用文本或整数（最小单位），避免浮点；时间戳 `INTEGER`（ms）；大字段 JSON 存 `TEXT`。

```sql
-- 0. 元信息
PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;

-- 1. 用户（最小）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
  id                TEXT PRIMARY KEY,            -- uuid
  address           TEXT,                        -- 可空
  created_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  status            TEXT DEFAULT 'active'        -- active|blocked
);

-- 2. API 密钥（可选持久化；若不用就不建）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_keys (
  id                TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  user_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
  exchange          TEXT NOT NULL,               -- 'okx'
  key_alias         TEXT,                        -- 建议：alias 模式使用
  enc_payload       TEXT NOT NULL,               -- AES-GCM 加密JSON：{apiKey,secretKey,passphrase,uid}
  created_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  last_used_at      INTEGER,
  scope             TEXT DEFAULT 'readonly',
  FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_api_keys_user ON api_keys(user_id);

-- 3. 验证请求日志（输入侧）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verify_requests (
  id                TEXT PRIMARY KEY,            -- uuid
  user_id           TEXT,
  exchange          TEXT NOT NULL,               -- 'okx'
  inst_id           TEXT NOT NULL,               -- 'BTC-USDT-SWAP'
  ord_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
  key_mode          TEXT NOT NULL,               -- inline|alias
  key_alias         TEXT,
  client_meta       TEXT,                        -- JSON
  flags_json        TEXT,                        -- {live,fresh,noCache,includeRaw,includePerf}
  requested_at      INTEGER NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_verify_req_ord ON verify_requests(exchange, inst_id, ord_id);

-- 4. 验证结果提要（归一化+索引字段）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS verifications (
  id                TEXT PRIMARY KEY,            -- uuid
  request_id        TEXT NOT NULL,
  order_id_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,               -- keccak256(exchange||instId||ordId||uid?)
  evidence_id       TEXT NOT NULL,               -- evi_*
  evidence_root     TEXT NOT NULL,
  exchange          TEXT NOT NULL,
  inst_id           TEXT NOT NULL,
  ord_id            TEXT NOT NULL,
  state             TEXT NOT NULL,               -- created|partially_filled|filled|canceled|failed
  side              TEXT,                        -- buy|sell
  pos_side          TEXT,                        -- long|short|BOTH
  lever             TEXT,
  mode              TEXT,                        -- isolated|cross
  liq_px            TEXT,
  adl               INTEGER DEFAULT 0,           -- 0/1
  liquidated        INTEGER NOT NULL,            -- 0/1
  liquidated_at     INTEGER,                     -- ms
  reason            TEXT,                        -- forced-liquidation|adl|manual-close|unknown
  mark_price        TEXT,
  verified_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  perf_total_ms     INTEGER,
  perf_okx_ms       INTEGER,
  source            TEXT NOT NULL,               -- live|cache
  FOREIGN KEY(request_id) REFERENCES verify_requests(id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_verifications_key ON verifications(exchange, inst_id, ord_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_verifications_hash ON verifications(order_id_hash);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_verifications_time ON verifications(verified_at);

-- 5. 证据包（大字段落盘 + 元数据）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence_blobs (
  evidence_id       TEXT PRIMARY KEY,            -- evi_*
  order_id_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,
  root              TEXT NOT NULL,
  parent_root       TEXT,
  leaves_count      INTEGER NOT NULL,
  bundle_hash       TEXT NOT NULL,
  blob_path         TEXT NOT NULL,               -- reports/evidence/2025-11-04/evi_xxx.json
  issued_at         INTEGER NOT NULL
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_evidence_hash ON evidence_blobs(order_id_hash);

-- 6. 保单（理赔前置；现在可选，之后接入）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS policies (
  policy_id         TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  buyer_user_id     TEXT,
  sku_id            TEXT NOT NULL,               -- '24h'|'8hWindow'|'monthlyDrawdown'|'noLiqCashback'
  principal         TEXT NOT NULL,               -- USDC
  leverage          INTEGER NOT NULL,
  payout_mode       TEXT NOT NULL,               -- fixed|ratio
  payout_fixed      TEXT,                        -- USDC
  payout_rate       TEXT,                        -- "0.6"
  premium_paid      TEXT NOT NULL,
  risk_coeff        TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '1.0',
  coverage_start    INTEGER NOT NULL,
  coverage_end      INTEGER NOT NULL,
  terms_version     TEXT NOT NULL,
  chain_id          INTEGER,
  usdc              TEXT,
  tx_hash           TEXT,
  created_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY(buyer_user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);

-- 7. 理赔（状态机）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS claims (
  claim_id          TEXT PRIMARY KEY,            -- clm_*
  policy_id         TEXT NOT NULL,
  user_id           TEXT,
  order_id_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,
  evidence_root     TEXT NOT NULL,
  proof_hash        TEXT NOT NULL,
  payout_amount     TEXT NOT NULL,               -- 与合约单位一致
  status            TEXT NOT NULL,               -- prepared|signed|rejected|paid
  reason            TEXT,
  nonce             INTEGER NOT NULL,
  deadline          INTEGER NOT NULL,
  created_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  decided_at        INTEGER,
  tx_hash           TEXT,
  FOREIGN KEY(policy_id) REFERENCES policies(policy_id),
  FOREIGN KEY(user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_claims_hash ON claims(order_id_hash);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_claims_status ON claims(status);

-- 8. 上链 attestation（可选）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attested_roots (
  root              TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  parent_root       TEXT,
  order_id_hash     TEXT NOT NULL,
  attested_at       INTEGER NOT NULL,
  tx_hash           TEXT
);

-- 9. 赔付记录
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payouts (
  id                TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  claim_id          TEXT NOT NULL,
  to_address        TEXT NOT NULL,
  amount            TEXT NOT NULL,
  token             TEXT NOT NULL,               -- USDC
  paid_at           INTEGER NOT NULL,
  tx_hash           TEXT NOT NULL,
  FOREIGN KEY(claim_id) REFERENCES claims(claim_id)
);

-- 10. 审计日志（轻量）
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs (
  id                TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  topic             TEXT NOT NULL,               -- verify|claim|attest|error|admin
  ref_id            TEXT,                        -- 关联ID
  created_at        INTEGER NOT NULL,
  payload_json      TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_topic_time ON audit_logs(topic, created_at);
```

**保留索引即刻生效；JSON 字段都可直接 `TEXT` 存储，必要时加虚拟列提速。**

---

# 3）OKX → 归一化字段映射（核心）

| 归一化字段 | OKX来源 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `normalized.order.type` | `trade/order.data[].ordType` | market/limit/post_only/ioc/fok |
| `normalized.order.side` | `trade/order.data[].side` | buy/sell |
| `normalized.order.state` | `trade/order.data[].state` | 统一映射：`canceled`/`filled`/`partially_filled`/`live→created`/`failed` |
| `normalized.order.px` | `trade/order.data[].px` | 价格 |
| `normalized.order.sz` | `trade/order.data[].sz` | 下单数量 |
| `normalized.order.accFillSz` | `trade/order.data[].accFillSz` | 累计成交 |
| `normalized.order.avgFillPx` | `trade/order.data[].avgPx` | 成交均价 |
| `normalized.order.fee` | `trade/order.data[].fee` | 手续费 |
| `normalized.order.ts` | `trade/order.data[].cTime/uTime` | 取 `uTime` 优先 |
| `normalized.position.lever` | `account/positions.data[].lever` | 杠杆 |
| `normalized.position.mode` | `account/positions.data[].mgnMode` | isolated/cross |
| `normalized.position.posSide` | `account/positions.data[].posSide` | long/short/… |
| `normalized.position.liqPx` | `account/positions.data[].liqPx` | 强平价 |
| `normalized.position.adl` | `account/positions.data[].adl` | 0→false, 1→true |
| `marketSnapshot.markPrice` | `public/ticker.data[].last` 或 `markPx` | 标记价/最新价 |
| `meta.instId` | `trade/order.data[].instId` | 校验与入参一致 |

**`liquidated` 判定建议（本地规则，可落代码）：**
1. 若当前 `positions` 对应合约 `pos=0` 且最近一段时间内存在被动平仓/强平记录 → `forced-liquidation`。  
2. 或 `adl=1` 且仓位被动减仓至 0 → `adl`。  
3. 仅 `liqPx` 被触达但仓位仍在 → `unknown`（不判定为 liquidated）。  
> 本地阶段可以用**订单+仓位快照+标记价**近似；严格版本再加账单/成交明细补证据。

---

# 4）证据包规范（落盘 + 上链可溯源）

- `evidence.orderIdHash = keccak256(exchange||instId||ordId||uid?)`
- `leaves` 覆盖三类：  
  ① 订单核心字段（type/side/state/accFillSz/avgFillPx/px/sz）  
  ② 仓位快照（lever/mode/posSide/liqPx/adl/liquidated/liquidatedAt/reason）  
  ③ 行情快照（markPrice/indexPrice/ts）
- `bundleHash` = 对**去敏**后的响应整体（删除密钥、IP、时序抖动字段）做 `keccak256`
- 落盘路径：`reports/evidence/YYYY-MM-DD/evi_*.json`（与 `evidence_blobs.blob_path` 对应）
- 同时写一份 `.root` 纯文本仅含 `evidence_root`

**evi_*.json 示例骨架**
```json
{
  "meta": { "exchange":"okx", "instId":"BTC-USDT-SWAP", "ordId":"…", "verifiedAt":"…" },
  "normalized": { "order":{…}, "position":{…}, "marketSnapshot":{…} },
  "raw": { "trade/order":{…}, "account/positions":{…}, "public/ticker":{…} },
  "evidence": { "orderIdHash":"0x…", "leaves":[{"k":"…","v":"…","ts":…}], "root":"0x…", "bundleHash":"0x…" }
}
```

---

# 5）状态机与错误码（最小可执行）

- **claims.status**：`prepared → signed → paid`；拒绝：`rejected`
- **错误码**
  - `AUTH_ERROR`：密钥/权限问题
  - `NOT_FOUND`：ordId/instId 不存在或不归属
  - `TIMEOUT`：所端超时
  - `REMOTE_4XX|REMOTE_5XX`：上游错误
  - `RATE_LIMIT`：频率限制
  - `RULE_DENY`：规则拒绝（prepare 阶段）

---

# 6）落地顺序（今天能跑）

1. **建库**：执行上面 DDL。  
2. **实现 `/api/verify`**：按映射取 OKX 三个端点（订单 / 仓位 / 行情），填充 `normalized`，生成 `evidence`，写 `verifications + evidence_blobs + audit_logs`。  
3. **（可选）实现 `/claims/prepare` 与 `/claims/voucher`**：把规则与签名走通，写 `claims`。  
4. **前端“订单验证器”**：表单（密钥/instId/ordId）→ 调 `/api/verify` → 展示 `normalized.position.liquidated`、`evidence.root`，附“查看证据包”。
